# Catalog creation implementation review

Reviewed 2026-10-24. Final independent four-file test review: **Approved**, no consequential findings. This report does not authorize integration, commit, push, developer-data migration or deployment.

The implementation is supported by inspected source, focused regression assertions, recorded isolated test runs and actual root browser checks. **Full conformance is not established:** the explicit runtime gaps and existing filter-scan exception below remain visible. No new unresolved production defect was found in the bounded final source review.

## Final full-suite verification and commit readiness — 2026-10-24

The user subsequently authorized the full isolated suites and a feature-only commit if they pass, followed by a separate merge decision. Root ran all configured suites; this supplements the earlier bounded audit and does not change its historical evidence levels or the 1,155-row ledger.

| Suite | Isolated target | Final result |
|---|---|---|
| Complete standard PHP suite (`catalog_test php artisan test --compact`) | Verified `testing` / SQLite `:memory:`; array cache/mail/session and synchronous queue; empty database URL overrides | **1,250 passed, 8,427 assertions**; 96.05 seconds |
| Complete dedicated MySQL suite (`vendor/bin/pest --configuration=phpunit.mysql.xml`) | Resolved and live-checked disposable `configurator_catalog_test` on `127.0.0.1:3307`, test-only account; no competing test-account connection | **45 passed, 335 assertions**; 4.04 seconds |
| Complete JavaScript suite (`npm test`) | Isolated worktree | **230 passed; 0 failed, skipped or cancelled** |
| PHP formatting (`vendor/bin/pint --dirty --format agent`) | Isolated worktree | Passed |

The first full PHP run identified six failures: two caused by the worktree missing the existing ignored private corpus, and four stale UI assertions in three existing test files. Root copied only the existing hash-pinned corpus (`5a8adbd4439bb1f020ab625d84fa6fde3a62ce67fbd74f94cf2b2e3b19758f72`) into ignored test storage. The three test files now exercise the agreed Default column, current nested inclusion state/local preview, and mounted batch Create slide-over. They preserve identity, local-draft and shared-record non-mutation coverage. No tests were deleted or skipped. The focused run passed 33 tests / 227 assertions; an independent reviewer approved the exact delta. No production changes were needed after the previous source freeze; all original 87 reviewed paths remain unchanged, with these three reviewed test updates added.

Root logs are retained in the ignored task evidence directory: `final-full-php.log` (initial failures), `final-full-php-rerun.log`, `final-full-mysql.log`, `final-full-node.log` and `final-full-pint.log`. The existing successful production build and FilaCheck evidence remain applicable because production source did not change during this final verification.

**Remaining verification is bounded and nonblocking for the requested commit:** actual hardware IME remains a useful manual follow-up on the user's normal input method; source guards and synthetic composition tests do not establish a hardware pass. Exhaustively multiplying all interactions by all workspaces and viewport sizes is not justified by an observed defect after representative browser coverage. Native maximum/loading/disabled selection combinations remain a disclosed follow-up; the adapter delegates to the native checkbox and has no demonstrated limit bypass. An independent read-only reviewer agreed with these dispositions. NV1, NV2 and NV4 remain Not verified in the ledger; no exhaustive or full-conformance claim is made. Existing EX1 filter-scan exceptions and the unmeasured 500-row nested capacity remain disclosed.

Only the reviewed feature source, the three corrected tests and eight named feature planning/audit documents are eligible for this commit. Private fixtures, environment files, generated assets, ignored evidence, skill edits and other tasks' documents are excluded. Merge, push, developer/live migration and deployment remain separate; the primary checkout's unrelated dirty files and current master must be preserved.

## Measurements and limits

Current nested check additionally measured 14,573B/depth 8/3 total components with exactly one picker (R5); this is a small nesting check, not maximum capacity. Current ten-row name edits: 7,592–8,772 request bytes, 366,514–371,865 response bytes, 1 component,maximum changed-property depth 6,10 measured responses plus1 released unmeasured response. Response-stage interception perturbed cadence/timing: no natural request-count, latency or throughput claim. Historical multi-Attribute/picker/submit byte measurements remain labeled Task2's bundle. Current PHP measurements separately record empty, ten Masters,3 Attributes/11 associations, picker A→B→A and submit workloads; native set(array) creates one helper HTTP request per path, not one browser request. The 500 raw/domain guard and installed limits are not evidence of maximum populated nested runtime capacity. That maximum remains unmeasured, as the specification explicitly permits reporting separately.
